### [1] [Alexander Torsion and Floer Comparisons in the BV-BFV Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30192)
*Nima Moshayedi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将相对阿贝尔BF挠率与纽结Floer同调的Alexander分次欧拉示性数进行比较，通过环面边界条件将归一化相对挠率与Alexander多项式关联，并在尖锐亏格界下由单个素循环挠率值恢复其全部系数；进一步计算了界面收缩与标量比较障碍，构造了六生成元dg分解以建立完美模范畴等价，并讨论了BV-BFV/Floer几何等价的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立BF理论（特别是Abelian BF模型）与低维拓扑不变量（如Alexander多项式、Floer同调）之间的精确联系，探索量子场论结构（如BV-BFV框架）与Floer理论间的范畴等价可能性。

Method: 运用相对挠率理论、Alexander分次同调、dg代数分解、Hochschild上同调、BV-BFV量子化框架及非共振多项式量子BFV扇区分析；结合环面代数、torus algebra的两扇区约化、Weyl可观测量与真空态还原等技术。

Result: 证明归一化相对Abelian BF挠率等于Alexander多项式；在尖锐亏格界下，单个素循环挠率值可完全确定该多项式；获得六生成元dg分解诱导的完美模范畴等价，保持导出配对；在非共振BFV扇区中，状态与Weyl可观测量均退化为真空场，但Hochschild不变量阻碍其与零次环面代数的Morita等价。

Conclusion: 尽管揭示了BF挠率与Alexander多项式/ Floer同调之间深刻而精细的对应关系，并构建了范畴层面的部分结构（如dg等价、BV-BFV实现），但未实现完整的几何BV-BFV/Floer等价；主要障碍体现于Hochschild不变量与两扇区配对缺陷。

Abstract: We compare relative Abelian BF torsion with the Alexander-graded Euler characteristic of knot Floer homology. A meridional annulus boundary condition identifies the normalized relative torsion with the Alexander polynomial. One exact prime-cyclotomic torsion value recovers all its coefficients under a sharp genus bound, compatibly with connected-sum gluing and branched-cover formulas. We compute interface contractions and obstructions to scalar comparisons, including the pairing defect of two-sector reduction for the degree-zero torus algebra. A six-generator dg resolution yields an equivalence of perfect-module categories preserving derived pairings and admits a classical one-dimensional BV-BFV realization. In the fixed nonresonant polynomial quantum BFV sector, over characteristic zero with $\hbar$ inverted, both states and Weyl observables reduce to a vacuum field; a Hochschild invariant obstructs a Morita equivalence with the degree-zero torus algebra. These results do not establish a full geometric BV-BFV/Floer equivalence.

</details>


### [2] [On Borodin-Gorin presentation of Heckman-Opdam $\mathfrak{gl}_n$ hypergeometric function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30304)

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Borodin-Gorin关于Heckman-Opdam gl_n超几何函数的交错积分表示与arXiv:2508.18864中研究的另外两种积分表示等价，并探讨了相关Baxter算子及从Ruijsenaars双曲模型出发的极限过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不同Heckman-Opdam超几何函数积分表示之间的等价性，统一理解其代数与可积结构；连接随机矩阵、特殊函数与量子可积系统。

Method: 利用解析延拓、积分变换与对称函数理论，结合Ruijsenaars模型的量子化框架和Baxter Q-算子技术，进行显式积分恒等式验证与极限分析。

Result: 确立Borodin-Gorin交错积分表示与arXiv:2508.18864中两种积分表示的严格等价性；给出对应Baxter算子的显式构造及其在Ruijsenaars双曲极限下的行为。

Conclusion: Heckman-Opdam gl_n超几何函数的多种积分表示具有深层可积结构性质，其统一描述通过Baxter算子与Ruijsenaars极限得以实现，为Macdonald/Heckman-Opdam理论与随机矩阵、共形场论的交叉提供新工具。

Abstract: We prove that Borodin-Gorin presentation for Heckman-Opdam $\mathfrak{gl}_n$ hypergeometric function by interlacing integrals is equivalent to two other integral presentations studied in arXiv:2508.18864. We also consider related Baxter operators and the corresponding limiting procedure from the Ruijsenaars hyperbolic model.

</details>

Abstract: We deduce the rational analogue of the elliptic Rains identity of type $A_n$ arXiv:0309252. We also derive reductions of the rational analogue and present the connection of them with well-known integrals in literature.

</details>


### [4] [Spin-Clifford Bundles and Spin Connections on the Hyperbolic Clifford Algebra - Part I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30542)
*E. Notte-Cuello,J. M. Hoff da Silva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了与双曲Clifford代数ℝᴴ=Cl(H_V)相关的旋量场理论，其中H_V = V⊕V*为双曲空间；构造了实本原幂等元、双曲手征分解与双曲旋量群；证明任意定向流形上存在典范双曲旋量结构（无额外拓扑障碍）；定义了各向同性联络扩张及相应的双曲旋量联络，并建立其相容性、曲率与显式形式，为后续双曲Clifford代数上的Dirac算子奠定几何基础。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 运用超凯莱-狄拉克代数表示论、实Clifford代数分类、理想生成与旋量模构造；结合微分几何中旋量结构存在性理论、联络扩张技术（各向同性延拓）及旋量联络的协变微分形式推导。

Result: 1) 构造出在复化下保持本原性的实本原幂等元，生成Witten超场理想；2) 给出双曲手征分解与双曲旋量群Spinh(V⊕V*)的明确代数刻画；3) 证明任意定向流形M上存在典范双曲旋量结构，对应旋量丛、Spin-Clifford丛及理想旋量子丛；4) 定义TM⊕T*M上的各向同性联络扩张及其诱导的双曲旋量联络，确立其良定义性、度量相容性、曲率公式与局部表达式。

Conclusion: 双曲Clifford代数上的旋量几何具有比Lorentzian情形更优的代数稳定性（幂等元本原性保持）与更弱的拓扑约束（仅需定向性），所建立的双曲旋量联络为定义自然双曲Dirac算子提供了完备且内蕴的几何框架。

Abstract: We develop the theory of spinor fields associated with the hyperbolic Clifford algebra $\mathbb{R}^{H}=\mathcal{C}\ell(H_V)$, which is associated with hyperbolic space $H_V=V\oplus V^{*}$. Algebraically, we exhibit an explicit real primitive idempotent which, unlike the Lorentzian case, remains primitive after complexification and generates the ideal of Witten superfields. We also describe the chirality decomposition and the hyperbolic spin group. Geometrically, we construct a canonical hyperbolic spin structure on every oriented manifold, showing that no topological obstructions arise beyond orientability and we study the associated spinor bundles, spin-Clifford bundles and ideal spinor subbundles. Finally, we introduce the isotropic extension of an arbitrary linear connection to $TM\oplus T^*M$, which is always compatible with the hyperbolic metric, and the associated hyperbolic spin connection, whose well-definedness, compatibilities, curvature and explicit form are established, providing the geometric framework for Dirac operators on the hyperbolic Clifford algebra, which we will present in a forthcoming article.

</details>


### [5] [First-order differential constraints for the nonlinear wave equation:wave fronts, admissible nonlinearities and exact solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30629)
*Oleg Kaptsov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过线性确定方程法构造三维非线性波动方程的精确解，引入一阶约束函数，导出微分约束并积分得ansatz，再代入原方程求解；所得解通常不具Lie点对称性；非线性形式由约束几何决定：特征超平面上任意、光锥上为三次或指数型、线源柱面前锋上为五次；解包括正弦-戈登壁、任意形变前缘的涡旋构型、任意轨迹运动点源场（含三次方程的非线性Larmor辐射功率公式），以及具有任意指定环面节点数的光滑有限能量三次方程解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展超越Lie对称方法的系统性构造技术，以获取更广泛、非对称、物理意义明确的非线性波动方程精确解。

Method: 线性确定方程法（Linear Determining Equation Method）：求解一阶约束函数满足的线性偏微分方程，将其设为零得到微分约束，积分约束得试探解（ansatz），再代入原非线性波动方程确定容许非线性与解结构。

Result: 获得多类新精确解：(i) 光滑有界正弦-戈登型畴壁与任意前缘形变的涡旋；(ii) 三次与Liouville型点源场，含运动轨迹任意的源及其精确辐射功率（三次情形下为非线性Larmor公式）；(iii) 具任意指定环面节点数的光滑有限能量三次方程解；(iv) 非线性项受约束几何严格限定：特征超平面任意、光锥上为f(φ)∝φ³或e^φ、柱面前锋上为φ⁵。

Conclusion: 线性确定方程法是构造高维非线性波动方程非对称精确解的有效普适框架，揭示了非线性结构与时空几何约束（如光锥、柱面）之间的内在对应关系，并产生物理上丰富的解族，涵盖孤子、辐射场与拓扑结构。

Abstract: We construct exact solutions of the nonlinear wave equation in three space dimensions by the method of linear determining equations: first-order constraint functions are found as solutions of the linear determining equation, the differential constraints obtained by setting them equal to zero are integrated, and the resulting ansatz is substituted into the equation. Lie point symmetries are a special case, and the solutions obtained generically have no symmetry. The determining equations tie the admissible nonlinearity to the geometry of the constraint: arbitrary on characteristic hyperplanes, cubic on light cones, and, for a pair of constraints, exponential on light cones and quintic on the cylindrical fronts of a line source. Among the solutions are smooth bounded sine-Gordon walls and vortex configurations whose shape varies arbitrarily along a front, the fields of point sources moving on arbitrary trajectories for the cubic and Liouville equations, with an exact nonlinear analogue of the Larmor formula for the power radiated in the cubic case, and smooth finite-energy solutions of the cubic equation with any prescribed number of toroidal nodal surfaces.

</details>


### [6] [A sharp gradient-corrected Lieb--Thirring inequality in one dimension with applications to density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30887)
*Thiago Carvalho Corso,Louis Garrigue*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一维情形下带半经典常数和梯度修正的尖锐Lieb-Thirring不等式，并在周期情形下引入了一个新的宇称依赖壳修正项；刻画了这些不等式的极值函数构成的二维‘庞加莱密度流形’；进而将该流形用于分析TFW动能泛函近似的误差，得到非渐近、显式的全局与局域误差估计，并建立了Kohn-Sham势与逆TFW势在对偶Sobolev范数下的收敛性及最优N^{-2}收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Thomas-Fermi-von Weizsäcker (TFW)动能泛函在密度泛函理论中的近似精度，建立严格、非渐近、几何结构清晰的误差控制框架，特别是揭示其精确成立的临界流形结构。

Method: 运用变分分析与谱理论方法证明sharp Lieb-Thirring不等式；通过欧拉-拉格朗日方程与对称性分析刻画极值密度；构造Poincaré密度流形作为TFW泛函的零误差集；利用密度到该流形的距离作为误差度量，结合Sobolev嵌入与对偶估计控制Kohn-Sham势差。

Result: 1) 一维sharp Lieb-Thirring不等式含梯度修正与周期壳修正项；2) 极值密度构成二维Poincaré密度流形；3) TFW动能误差被距离×N显式控制，且在流形上精确为零；4) 周期Kohn-Sham势与逆TFW势在H^{-1}型对偶范数下误差受密度到流形距离控制；5) 高粒子数极限下收敛速率为最优的O(N^{-2})。

Conclusion: Poincaré密度流形是TFW近似精确成立的自然几何结构；该结构不仅提供紧致误差估计，还揭示了Kohn-Sham势在高密度极限下的最优收敛行为，为密度泛函理论的数学基础提供了关键几何视角。

Abstract: We first prove a sharp Lieb-Thirring inequality with semi-classical constant and gradient correction in one dimension. In the periodic setting, the corresponding inequality also includes a parity dependent shell-correction term, which appears to be new. Moreover, we also characterize the optimizers of these inequalities. In particular, the optimizing density profiles form a two-dimensional manifold, which we call here the Poincaré density manifold. We then apply these results to study the Thomas--Fermi--von Weizsäcker (TFW) approximation of the kinetic energy in Density Functional Theory. More precisely, we first obtain an explicit and non-asymptotic bound on the global and local error made by the TFW approximation. These bounds are controlled by the distance of the normalized density profile from the Poincaré density manifold times the number of particles. In particular, these bounds show that the TFW approximation is exact on the Poincaré manifold. We then show that, in the periodic setting, the distance between the Kohn-Sham potential and the inverse TFW potential in the natural dual Sobolev space can also be controlled by explicit density dependent constants times the distance to the Poincaré manifold. As a corollary, we prove that for a fixed density profile, the Kohn-Sham potential converges towards the inverse Thomas--Fermi potential in the high-density limit $N \rightarrow \infty$ with asymptotic rate of order $N^{-2}$. This rate is shown to be optimal.

</details>

*A. Ibort,M. Leok,R. Salmoni,C. Sardón*

Conclusion: Dirac结构为最优控制的对称性约化与约束处理提供了统一、内蕴且坐标无关的几何语言；Atiyah代数胚是描述对称控制系统的自然相空间；曲率作为联络几何对象，在异常性分析中起核心作用；该框架兼容正则与非正则、正常与异常两类极值问题。

Abstract: We study the symmetry reduction of optimal control problems using Dirac structures. The first-order conditions of the Pontryagin Maximum Principle are written as an implicit Dirac system on the Pontryagin bundle. For a problem invariant under a Lie group, we reduce both the Lagrange-Pontryagin variational principle and the underlying Dirac structure, and prove that they yield the same reduced equations, which we identify with the Hamiltonian equations on the dual of the Atiyah algebroid. Reconstruction, independence of the principal connection, and the relation between criticality and optimality complete the picture. Dropping regularity, we give the Gotay-Nester-Hinds constraint algorithm a Dirac-geometric form and prove that symmetry reduction commutes with it. For control-linear systems whose admissible directions are horizontal for a principal connection, abnormal extremals lie on the leaf where the base costate vanishes, and the first secondary constraint is the degeneracy of the curvature pairing. We also give a reduced criterion for strict abnormality and a metric-free index form. Examples: a spacecraft with rotors (flat connection), the Heisenberg group (nowhere-zero curvature) and the Martinet problem (curvature vanishing on a hypersurface).

</details>

Abstract: We consider the quantization of linearized Einstein equations on 4-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetimes. In this work, we extend two of such basic tools, cumulants and Wick polynomials, to a setting where the random variables no longer need to commute, with the goal of handling expectations of creation and annihilation operators on a Fock space, both in the bosonic and fermionic case. We call these constructions symmetric and antisymmetric cumulants and Wick polynomials, respectively. As an application, we derive a hierarchy of evolution equations satisfied by such cumulants, usually called truncated correlation functions in this context, for a gas of bosons or fermions hopping on a finite lattice and interacting via a two-body potential. With sufficient control over solutions to these hierarchies, one could control the magnitude of the error terms related to an approximation of the state of the quantum system by a finite lattice kinetic equation studied in our earlier work, closely connected to standard bosonic and fermionic Boltzmann--Nordheim equations.

Abstract: For the classical model of a system of N particles with interaction (the N-body problem) we consider some spatial problems of nonlinear programming for finding conditional minima and local extrema for the main characteristics of the system on its phase space. We prove that these conditional minima and local extrema of the system characteristics can only be achieved on flat, in general, not necessarily finite, trajectories of the system in space, (under the condition that the system angular momentum is non-zero). And then we find the basic conditions limiting the values of the integrals of motion: angular momentum and total energy of the system under which these trajectories are flat finite ("circular" or elliptical) trajectories on which the flat structure of the system is preserved in time under the certain restrictions on the values of the system's integrals of motion. And we determine also the values of the system's key characteristics on these flat trajectories. Next, in the Appendix, we consider some additional related results for a more general model of the system.

Abstract: Large-volume neutrino telescopes such as IceCube and KM3NeT exploit natural ice or water as detection media, but their sparse instrumentation makes MeV neutrino interactions difficult to identify individually. We show that chlorine naturally abundant in seawater provides an intrinsic neutron-capture target that enables efficient tagging of electron antineutrinos through inverse beta decay. In contrast to the 2.2 MeV $\gamma$-ray from neutron capture on hydrogen, capture on chlorine produces an $\sim$8.6 MeV $\gamma$-ray cascade on a shorter time scale, yielding a distinctive delayed-coincidence signature. Using measured seawater properties at the TRIDENT site and a detector-level simulation, we find that chlorine-assisted tagging can extend the core-collapse supernova 5$\sigma$ discovery reach from $\sim$50 to over 100 kpc. We validate the underlying detection principle with a salted-water experiment at the China Jinping Underground Laboratory, observing correlated neutron-capture signatures under suppressed cosmic muon background conditions. Natural seawater thus transforms the deep ocean into an intrinsically enhanced antineutrino detection medium, opening new opportunities for MeV neutrino astronomy and distributed measurements of reactor and geophysical antineutrinos.

Conclusion: CURTAIN是首个满足全部三项核心要求的中微子望远镜SSL任务，验证了时序结构建模对高能中微子事件重建的关键作用，并为多探测器联合分析提供了可迁移表征基础。

Abstract: Neutrino telescopes record more than a trillion photon detections per year, yet there does not exist a self-supervised learning (SSL) task which fully satisfies all three conditions: improving as the pretraining set grows, transferring to a different detector, and outperforming the best model trained without pretraining at all. We introduce CURTAIN, a temporal masking scheme and the first SSL task for neutrino telescopes that achieves all of these goals. Downstream performance monotonically improves with the number of pretraining events, as predicted by scaling laws. Our encoder builds on DeepIce, currently the leading model on the NuBench benchmarks direction reconstruction task. Pretraining it with CURTAIN reduces its median angular error by 45% and its median energy error by 31% compared to training from scratch. It also transfers to a detector geometry never seen in pretraining, reducing the median angular error by 36% compared to training from scratch. At matched capacity and pretraining data, CURTAIN-pretrained encoders beat those pretrained by existing SSL tasks on both tasks and at every pretraining set size.

Abstract: We present a study of the three-body, singly Cabibbo-suppressed decays $\Lambda_c^+ \to \Lambda K^+ \pi^0$ and $\Lambda_c^+ \to \Lambda K^+ \eta$ using data recorded with the Belle and Belle II detectors at the KEKB and SuperKEKB $e^+ e^-$ colliders. The data samples correspond to integrated luminosities of 980$\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ and 570$\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, respectively. We measure their branching-fraction ratios relative to the Cabibbo-favored decay $\Lambda_c^+ \to \Lambda \pi^+ \pi^0$ as $\mathcal{B}(\Lambda_c^+ \to \Lambda K^+ \pi^0)/\mathcal{B}(\Lambda_c^+ \to \Lambda \pi^+ \pi^0)=(1.82 \pm 0.07 \pm 0.07)\%$, with significantly improved precision compared to the previous measurement, and $\mathcal{B}(\Lambda_c^+ \to \Lambda K^+ \eta)/\mathcal{B}(\Lambda_c^+ \to \Lambda \pi^+ \pi^0)=(8.73 \pm 0.35 \pm 0.43)\times10^{-3}$, corresponding to the first observation of the $\Lambda_c^+ \to \Lambda K^+ \eta$ decay with a statistical significance exceeding 10 standard deviations. Prototype measurements demonstrate a quality factor of $Q\simeq 5\times 10^5$, while $Q=10^7$ is considered as a future development target. Under the assumed flux coupling and an idealized quantum-limited SQUID noise model, optimized circuits yield projected strain sensitivities of order $10^{-21}\,\mathrm{Hz}^{-1/2}$ over $100\,\mathrm{Hz}$ bands in the $1$--$20\,\mathrm{kHz}$ range. Increasing the active high-field volume and improving quantum-limited readout could enable further sensitivity gains. This compact, tunable detector concept could complement interferometric gravitational-wave searches and motivate investigations of high-frequency astrophysical sources.

Abstract: We show that $^{229}$Th nuclear clocks can probe departures from the Peccei-Quinn (PQ) solution to the strong CP problem. Abstract: We investigate the prospects for improving the heavy neutral lepton (HNL) searches at the far-forward LHC experiments FASER and FASER$\nu$, as well as their proposed HL-LHC successors, FASER2 and FASER$\nu$2, using new HNL production mechanisms. We further develop a novel measurement-record formalism, which computes the joint probability distribution of all measurement outcomes and is well suited to studying the axion in the time domain. With it, we compute the exact axion-induced number distribution for the first time, demonstrating that it is not Poisson. We argue that virialized axion dark matter is expected to be in a Gaussian quantum state, and show that number or quadrature measurements can distinguish this from the predictions of a coherent state or even a Bose-Einstein condensate. Finally, we show that intrinsically quantum effects, such as those from Fock or squeezed axion states, are far too weak to detect.

Abstract: We study Neutrino Transition Polarizability, where a light pseudoscalar $\phi$ couples active neutrinos to heavy neutral leptons $N$ and interacts with two photons. Abstract: Neutrino-nucleus cross sections are essential inputs for long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiments, yet remain challenging to model with high precision. Recent work demonstrated that fully black-box data-driven cross-section models cannot accurately generalize between near- and far-detector fluxes, but imposing Standard-Model symmetries via structure-function parameterizations provides a well-posed learning problem in a simplified DUNE-inspired muon-neutrino disappearance analysis. We find that the learned cross section model recovers the injected oscillation parameters without significant bias, with constraints comparable to those obtained using the exact underlying cross section. Flux uncertainties have more significant effects in the multi-channel case, and incorporating flux nuisance parameters into the learning problem is required for accurate results. TL;DR: 本文通过闭合测试框架，比较了高斯过程、神经网络集成和经验神经正切核近似等机器学习方法在非单态三重态部分子分布函数（PDF）重建中的不确定性估计性能，强调了实验协方差、函数先验及核评估时机对不确定性校准的关键影响。

Abstract: Machine-learning methods are increasingly used to solve inverse problems in physics, where a faithful estimate of the uncertainty on the reconstructed function is as important as its central value. We address this question in the simplest instance of a parton distribution function (PDF) determination: the reconstruction of the non-singlet triplet distribution $T_3(x,Q_0^2)$ from deep-inelastic scattering data. Within a closure-test framework, in which the underlying PDF is known and the pseudo-data carry correlated experimental uncertainties, we compare Gaussian processes with different kernels, neural-network ensembles trained with different loss functions, and empirical neural tangent kernel approximations. We find that methods yielding comparable central reconstructions can produce markedly different uncertainties, and we identify the ingredients that control them: a likelihood that includes the full experimental covariance, the functional prior encoded in the kernel or in the endpoint preprocessing, and, for tangent-kernel approximations, the stage of training at which the kernel is evaluated. A normalised-bias diagnostic shows that calibration depends on the correlations of the predictive covariance, not only on its pointwise width. Our results illustrate why closure tests and covariance-aware diagnostics are essential to validate uncertainties in ML-based approaches to inverse problems.

The Dirac alternative removes these characteristic signals while retaining a relation between left- and right-handed flavor. The right-handed leptonic mixing matrix can then be reconstructed from low-energy data and the spontaneous parity-breaking parameter. Requiring the two lepton Yukawa matrices to admit a common real parity basis turns this reconstruction into a compatibility condition that determines the allowed Dirac phase on each branch. The small Dirac masses also restrict the parity deformation and its induced strong-CP phase. For compressed mixed-sign neutrino spectra, a tiny parity deformation can nevertheless generate large leptonic CP violation, despite stringent strong-CP constraints. Abstract: We report a matrix-free numerical pipeline for semiclassical false-vacuum decay in a four-component global-O(4) scalar theory in four Euclidean dimensions. The calculation combines continuum bounce shooting, action relaxation, channel-resolved fluctuation spectra, deflated stochastic Lanczos quadrature (SLQ), and a continuum partial-wave determinant with Gel'fand-Yaglom radial ratios and large-angular-momentum subtraction. For the benchmark potential, the bounce action is B = 145.203947 and the tail residual is 0.281955%. On an L = 26, h = 0.8 lattice, the relaxed field achieves an RMS EOM residual of 4.43 x 10^-12; its low spectrum contains one negative radial mode, four lattice translations, and three near-zero internal modes. The 16-probe bare SLQ trace-log is -2079.9042 +/- 6.4504. Under the subtraction prescription, the scalar partial-wave calculation returns a primed trace-log of 40.9324 at mu = mF = sqrt(0.4), giving a determinant factor of 1.2931 x 10^-9. We show the leading rho^8 potential is not closed under its scalar one-loop divergence: local rho^10 and rho^12 counterterms are required, leaving finite coefficients unmatched; hence this is a prescription-level result, not a complete EFT prediction. The translation-normalized scalar expression is 5.9974 x 10^-70 in lattice energy units to the fourth power, omitting the internal-orientation Jacobian. We summarize tested SU(2) operator and pole-algebra infrastructure, identifying finite full-gauge matching and controlled limits as open work.

The likelihood is usually estimated by Monte Carlo integration over finite sets of event posterior samples and simulated injections. Sharp population features can also lie in regions poorly sampled by the event posteriors. Increasing the sample count reduces Monte Carlo variance but leaves uncertainty from fitting the densities to finite training sets. We assess the physical viability and stability of the stellar configurations using the energy conditions (strong, weak, null, and dominant) and the speed of sound. Overall, the $f(R,T^2)$ gravity model can accommodate quark star configurations and reproduce the observed properties of massive compact objects, particularly those associated with GW190814 and the lightest compact object observed, HESS J1731-347, respectively.

Thus finite real $\ell$ covers precisely the acceleration interval $|A|<|B|$: for $\ell\ne0$ the corresponding OP acceleration parameter is nonzero and the two axial openings are unequal, although no acceleration horizon is present. Conclusion: HF-QPO数据无法独立区分电荷Q、AdS半径l与正则化尺度b；所提取的‘形变’实质是静态度规对Kerr自旋动力学的低阶有效吸收；该结果揭示了轨道频率模型中固有的参数简并，不构成对真实电荷或Λ的物理测量。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we study circular geodesics and twin-peak high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (HF-QPOs) in the charged Simpson--Visser--anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime, which depends on a charge parameter $Q$, a regularization scale $b$ and an AdS radius $l$. Written in terms of the areal radius $R=\sqrt{r^{2}+b^{2}}$, the specific energy, angular momentum, Keplerian frequency and innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) of test particles coincide with those of the Reissner--Nordström--AdS black hole, so $b$ enters the epicyclic spectrum only through a factor $1-b^{2}/R^{2}$ in the radial frequency. We give this frequency and the ISCO condition in closed form. We then fit the relativistic-precession model to the HF-QPO pairs of GRO~J1655$-$40 and XTE~J1550$-$564, with their dynamical masses included as Gaussian priors. The Schwarzschild limit gives $\chi^{2}=32.9$ for two degrees of freedom. One additional deformation removes this tension: $Q/M=0.814^{+0.059}_{-0.074}$ at fixed $l=100M$, or $l=40.6^{+6.2}_{-4.8}\,M$ at $Q=0$, both with $\chi^{2}\simeq0.13$. These two solutions lie on a single degeneracy valley in the $(Q,l)$ plane, and, at $l=100M$, $b$ is left unconstrained up to $b\simeq3.9M$ (68\%), a limit set by the charge prior rather than by the data. A Kerr black hole with spin $a\simeq0.3$--$0.4$ reproduces the same pairs, so the inferred deformation most likely absorbs the spin neglected by the static metric. Our results therefore describe degeneracies among orbital-frequency deformations; they are not a measurement of an electric charge or of a cosmological constant.

</details>

Abstract: This thesis investigates the perturbative dynamics and phenomenology of compact objects in two approaches to quantum gravity: quadratic gravity and asymptotic safety. Linear perturbations provide a tool to probe the physical properties of spacetimes and their potential observational signatures, including quasinormal modes, black hole shadows, and Hawking radiation. The first part focuses on Einstein-Weyl gravity, where an additional massive spin-2 degree of freedom modifies the dynamics of compact objects. We derive and analyze tensor perturbation for static, spherically symmetric solutions, identifying Gregory-Laflamme-type instabilities in the monopole sector and studying the time domain evolution of higher multipoles. We also investigate universal massive-field tails and the breakdown of the standard eikonal correspondence between quasinormal modes and null geodesics. Particular attention is devoted to veiled singularities, horizonless configurations that satisfy a form of weak cosmic censorship and can mimic black hole shadows. The second part examines regular black holes in asymptotically safe gravity. Using the proper-time flow approach, we construct and investigate a dynamical regular black hole solution arising from gravitational collapse. We analyze its perturbative stability and quasinormal mode spectrum, finding characteristic deviations from Schwarzschild black holes. We explore the phenomenological implications of quantum corrections for shadows and Hawking radiation. While shadow remains compatible with current constraints, the modified evaporation process features lower temperatures and suppressed emission rates, with implications for primordial black holes and their possible contribution to dark matter. Overall, this work demonstrates how the perturbative dynamics of compact objects can connect quantum gravity models to potentially observable astrophysical phenomena.

</details>

Abstract: We study the exact Onsager algebra of free lattice fermions in staggered, Wilson, and overlap incarnations, working in both Hamiltonian and Euclidean formulations in four spacetime dimensions. In each case, we show that the exact lattice algebra emanates the full chiral symmetry of the low-energy theory: if there are $N$ massless Dirac fermions in the continuum, the Onsager algebra reduces to a $U(2N)$ chiral symmetry. The details of which generators are quantised or ultra-local differ between formulations. There is also a trade-off between the locality of generators and the maximum compact subgroup of the Onsager algebra.

Abstract: We study perturbations of topological defects by a relevant holomorphic defect field in two-dimensional conformal field theory. The semiring homomorphism preserves functional relations, and we illustrate in the example of Virasoro minimal models that the resulting relations in the asymptotic semiring constrain the defect flow endpoints. The companion paper [1] contains a detailed study of such defect flows in several minimal models, both numerically and analytically.

Abstract: The Euclidean saddles of 3D Einstein gravity with positive cosmological constant are spherical three-manifolds. This leads to a spherical volume conjecture for the product of continued Virasoro TQFT amplitudes. In appropriate limits, we recover the static patch scalar two-point function with finite observer backreaction and the semiclassical effects of observer recoil. In addition, we analyze examples with knotted worldlines and reproduce their spherical volumes. We also apply our prescription to Seifert manifolds, which are off-shell in AdS$_3$ gravity but admit dS$_3$ saddles, and find agreement with their spherical volumes semiclassically. Finally, using Dehn surgery on torus knots and links, we obtain partition functions on smooth spherical three-manifolds.
