*Kazi Fahim Reza Arko,Walid Hasan,M. Fahim Hoque,Ahmed Rakin Kamal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了三个无质量IIA型超引力的解析双扭曲宇宙学解，对应不同内部方向通量（H3、F̃2、F̃4），证明了在无局域源、光滑紧致空间上可实现非平凡扭曲；其11维提升与4维爱因斯坦帧宇宙学显示大爆炸—最大尺度—大挤压演化；存在短暂尺度分离（|HE|/mKK ≪ 1），且全部破缺IIA型所有超对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无局域源下光滑紧致流形上非恒定warping的可能性，并检验其是否支持尺度分离与低能有效宇宙学描述。

Method: 构造doubly warped乘积时空Ansatz，求解massless type IIA supergravity场方程；计算11维M理论提升（通过S1纤维化）；将10维度规共形变换至4维爱因斯坦帧并推导Friedmann动力学；分析Kaluza–Klein质量谱与Hubble参数比值以检验scale separation；利用Killing旋量方程检验超对称性保留情况。

Result: 获得三个显式解析解，均具双扭曲结构；其4维宇宙学为闭合、减速膨胀-收缩演化（a(t)单峰）；存在瞬时尺度分离（仅在a(t)极大值附近满足|HE|/mKK ≪ 1）；所有解均无残留超对称（即SUSY-breaking background）。

Conclusion: 光滑、无源、紧致内空间上的非平凡warping可兼容标准超引力与经典宇宙学演化，但无法长期维持尺度分离，且必然破缺全部IIA超对称；此类背景不宜作为稳定de Sitter真空候选，但可用于研究奇点邻域的量子引力效应或SUSY-breaking机制。

Abstract: We construct three analytic, doubly warped cosmological solutions of massless type IIA supergravity with different choices of fluxes, namely, $H_3$, $\tilde F_2$ and $\tilde F_4$ along internal directions. The $H_3$ solution also belongs to the common NS--NS sector of type IIB. Our solutions show that, without localised sources, nonconstant warping on a smooth compact space is possible. We derive their eleven-dimensional uplifts and four-dimensional Einstein-frame cosmology. Each universe undergoes (decelerating) expansion from a Big Bang, reaches a maximum size, and undergoes contraction towards a Big Crunch. We also analyse the scale separation of these backgrounds and find that they exhibit a small Einstein frame Hubble scale to KK mass ratio $\frac{|H_E|}{m_{\mathrm{KK}}}\ll1,$ although for a brief period of time, around the maxima of the scale factor in the Einstein frame. Finally, we analyse the supersymmetry of these backgrounds, which demonstrates that all three solutions break all forms of type IIA supersymmetry.

</details>


### [100] [Pressure in Wilsonian effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30249)
*Vinícius Bernardes,Atakan Hilmi Fırat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Wilson有效场论中的指数滚落tachyon解，作为开弦场论中滚落tachyon的模型；发现其压力在有限时间内与底层裸理论一致，之后则由另一类具有过去振荡性、晚期正则性的解主导；据此推测开弦场论中tachyon滚落的晚期物理可能由非标准滚落解描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开弦场论中滚落tachyon的晚期动力学行为，尤其是其压力演化是否能由Wilsonian有效场论中的可解模型合理刻画。

Method: 在Wilsonian有效场论框架下构造并分析指数型滚落tachyon解，重点考察其能量-动量张量分量（特别是压力）的时间演化行为，并对比其与裸理论预言的一致性范围及失效机制。

Result: 该指数解在有限时间前压力与裸理论一致，此后压力演化由另一支解接管——该解在共形时间过去方向振荡、未来方向光滑正则；表明标准滚落解无法完整描述晚期物理。

Conclusion: 开弦场论中tachyon滚落的晚期渐近行为可能需借助非标准（如过去振荡、未来正则）的解来刻画，标准滚落解仅具有限时有效性。

Abstract: We study the exponential rolling tachyon solution in Wilsonian effective field theory as a model for rolling tachyons in open string field theory. Although this solution exhibits runaway oscillations towards the future similar to its counterparts in string field theory, its pressure is shown to be consistent with the underlying bare theory, but only up to a finite time. The rest of the evolution of the pressure is instead governed by a solution that is oscillatory towards the past and regular at late times. We conjecture that the late time physics of rolling tachyons in open string field theory may be captured by the solutions that are different from the standard rolling tachyon solutions based on this analysis.

</details>


### [101] [Fluids on null hypersurfaces and Carrollian hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30254)
*Emil Have*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入在时空中的零超曲面上的内禀理想流体及其在环境时空中的因果流体实现；构建了基于零生成元重标度诱导的时间Weyl对称性的Carroll结构与变分理论；分类了内禀Carroll流体相，指出零速环境流体对应零温、未破缺Carroll提升对称的内禀相，而类时/零速环境流体穿越零超曲面则导致开放Carroll系统；进一步发现存在一个无限Boost极限（保持焓有限非零），使类时环境流体退耦为自治Carroll流体，其Carroll提升对称自发破缺，并涌现出子主导速度分量所对应的独立Carroll提升Goldstone模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解零超曲面上Carroll对称性流体的动力学本质及其与环境广义相对论流体的对应关系，尤其关注零超曲面作为全息屏或黑洞视界时的低能有效描述。

Method: 发展零子流形微分几何与变分计算框架，引入由零生成元重标度导出的时间Weyl对称性；系统分析Carroll提升对称性与时间Weyl对称性的实现方式；通过极限分析（无限Boost保持焓有限）构造退耦极限，并提取诱导Carroll响应与Goldstone模。

Result: 1) 建立了嵌入零超曲面的Carroll结构及其变分原理；2) 分类了内禀Carroll流体相：零温未破缺相（对应纯零速环境流体）与开放系统相（对应穿越流体）；3) 发现一类自治Carroll流体可由类时环境流体在特定无限Boost极限下实现，其Carroll提升对称自发破缺，且存在独立的Carroll提升Goldstone激发。

Conclusion: 零超曲面上的Carroll流体并非仅限于零温极限，而可通过环境类时流体的可控退耦极限获得更丰富的动力学相，包括自发破缺Carroll对称性与涌现Goldstone模，为零超曲面热力学与全息对偶提供了新的微观实现路径。

Abstract: We study intrinsic ideal fluids on null hypersurfaces and their realisations by causal fluids living in the ambient spacetime. An embedded null hypersurface is equipped with an induced Carrollian structure with a temporal Weyl symmetry inherited from rescalings of the null generator, and we develop the corresponding null submanifold and variational calculus. The intrinsic fluid phases are classified according to their realisation of Carroll boosts and temporal Weyl symmetry. If we demand that an ambient fluid be confined to a null hypersurface, the ambient fluid velocity must be null, which realises an intrinsic zero-temperature Carrollian phase with unbroken Carroll boosts. In general, timelike or null ambient fluids will cross a null hypersurface and lead to an open Carrollian system. On a null foliation, the sources correspond to transport between null leaves. We then show that there exists a decoupling limit of a timelike ambient fluid which gives rise to an autonomous Carrollian fluid with spontaneously broken Carroll boosts and no source terms. This decoupling limit corresponds to an infinite boost while keeping the enthalpy finite and nonzero, which leads to a divergent ambient energy-momentum tensor. Nevertheless, the induced Carrollian responses do not see the divergent term and remain finite, and an independent Carroll boost Goldstone emerges from a subleading component of the ambient fluid velocity in this limit.

</details>


### [102] [Maxwell-Chern-Simons-Matter in AdS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30260)
*Dean Carmi,Riccardo Ciccone*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₃上耦合N个复标量或狄拉克费米子的Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论，包含sextic与Gross-Neveu相互作用；在大N极限下固定κ=k/N和α=Ne²，推导了光子传播子（精确至α, κ），分析其极点给出手征分裂的自旋-1边界谱，并提取共形点处的大N BCFT数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS₃中规范场与强耦合物质场（标量/费米子）在Chern-Simons拓扑项影响下的全息相结构与边界CFT谱，特别是手征电流的保护性与Higgs机制对其的影响。

Method: 采用大N展开，固定't Hooft耦合κ=k/N与电荷耦合α=Ne²；严格求解AdS₃中Maxwell-Chern-Simons传播子（区分平直与质量扇区）；对物质圈图进行重求和以获得精确至α, κ的光子传播子；通过传播子极点分析边界自旋-1谱；在共形点匹配BCFT算符谱与OPE系数。

Result: 得到标量与费米子相的完整相图；光子传播子极点给出两支手征分离的边界自旋-1模；Dirichlet边界下未Higgs相存在受保护的手征流，Higgs化后消失；费米子相中该流在非零有效CS水平下持续存在，手征性由诱导CS级数决定；提取critical/tricritical标量及regular fermion/Gross-Neveu理论的大N BCFT数据。

Conclusion: Chern-Simons项通过手征分裂与有效级数调控边界流的保护性，揭示了AdS₃中规范-物质耦合系统在Higgs相变与共形极限之间的深刻全息对应关系，为三维高阶BCFT提供了可计算范例。

Abstract: We study Maxwell-Chern-Simons theory on AdS$_3$ coupled to $N$ complex scalars or Dirac fermions, including sextic and Gross-Neveu interactions, respectively. First, we derive the bulk Maxwell-Chern-Simons propagator, clarifying how Dirichlet boundary conditions fix the contributions of the massive and flat sectors. Upon coupling to the matter sector, we work at large $N$ with $\kappa=k/N$ and $\alpha=Ne^2$ fixed, where $k$ is the Chern-Simons level. We determine the corresponding phase diagrams, and we resum matter insertions to obtain the photon propagator at leading order in large $N$, exactly in $\alpha$ and $\kappa$. Its poles determine the spin-one boundary spectrum, split into two helicity sectors by the Chern-Simons interaction. With Dirichlet boundary conditions, the unHiggsed scalar phase contains a protected chiral current, which is lifted by Higgsing. In the fermionic phases, the current persists for nonzero effective Chern-Simons level, with helicity determined by the induced level shift. At bulk conformal points, we extract large-$N$ BCFT data for the critical and tricritical scalar theories, as well as for the regular fermion and Gross-Neveu theories.

</details>


### [103] [A tale of three points: mixed and tensor bispectra beyond leading order in Horndeski gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30269)
*Y.A.Ageeva,M.A.Stepanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Horndeski理论框架下，首次在慢变近似一阶精度下完整推导了原初ζζh、ζhh和hhh三点半函数（bispectra），涵盖全部动量与螺旋度依赖性，并验证了张量软极限与标量软极限下的Maldacena一致性关系；同时指出当标量与张量声速不等时，软极限行为由无量纲比c_T q/(c_S k)主导，揭示声学交叉现象，并将结果应用于非奇异收缩宇宙模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解早期宇宙非高斯性特征对区分暴胀与替代模型（如收缩宇宙）至关重要；Horndeski理论作为最一般的二阶标量-张量引力理论，其原初非高斯性尚未在完整动量/螺旋度结构及慢变一阶精度下系统计算。

Method: 采用ADM分解与有效场论方法，在Horndeski理论中构造三阶作用量；精确求解一阶慢变修正下的标量（ζ）与张量（h）模函数演化；计入立方耦合系数的时间演化、尺度因子修正、功率谱归一化修正；显式计算所有三点半函数并分析软极限与有限挤压极限。

Result: 得到普适的⟨ζζh⟩、⟨ζhh⟩、⟨hhh⟩三点半函数解析表达式，含完整k_i依赖与helicity结构；确认Maldacena一致性关系在O(ε)慢变精度下成立；发现c_S ≠ c_T时软极限由c_T q/(c_S k)控制，导致声学交叉；构造出对应非线性参数f_NL^{ζζh}等，并应用于nonsingular contracting模型。

Conclusion: Horndeski理论中原初非高斯性具有丰富的动力学结构，其软极限行为超越纯几何压缩，由声速比决定；混合三点半函数可作为探测收缩相物理（如标量传播速度）的关键观测量。

Abstract: We derive the primordial $\langle\zeta\zeta h\rangle$, $\langle\zeta hh\rangle$, and $\langle hhh\rangle$ bispectra in general Horndeski theory through first order in slow variation, retaining their complete momentum and helicity dependence. The calculation includes the time evolution of the cubic coefficients, the correction to the scale factor, the first-order scalar and tensor mode functions, and the corrected power-spectrum normalization. We verify the tensor-soft and scalar-soft Maldacena consistency relations at the same order. We also study finite squeezing and show that, when $c_S\neq c_T$, the relevant soft variable is the ratio $c_Tq/(c_Sk)$, leading to an acoustic crossover not captured by geometric squeezing alone. We construct the corresponding nonlinearity parameters and apply the general results to a nonsingular contracting model, in which the mixed bispectra retain sensitivity to the contraction parameter through the scalar propagation speed.

</details>


### [104] [Data analysis of millions of Type IIB flux vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30273)
*Yuta Hamada,Yuma Uematsu,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIB型弦理论在加权射影空间$\mathbb{CP}^4_{1,1,1,6,9}$中18次超曲面上的通量紧化真空结构，通过大规模通量扫描与梯度优化识别数百万候选通量真空，并利用二次通量不变量作为特征训练分类器与回归模型，以预测超对称性破缺程度、标量势及模场期望值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解IIB弦理论中通量紧化的真空景观结构，特别是超对称（SUSY）与非超对称真空的统计分布、物理可观测量之间的相关性，以及通量不变量对低能有效物理的预测能力。

Method: 对满足D3-膜荷反对偶约束（tadpole bound）的3077万种整数通量组合进行系统扫描；采用基于SLSQP算法的带约束梯度最小化方法搜寻标量势的局域极小值；筛选出129.6万个高置信度解；以二次通量不变量为输入特征，构建分类器识别近似超对称真空（$|V|<10^{-4.5}$），并开展监督回归预测势能与各模场真空期望值。

Result: 分类器在前5%高分样本中可召回47.2%的真实近SUSY解；回归任务中，二次通量不变量显著提升标量势与复结构模场预测精度，而原始六维通量参数更优于轴子-稀子（axio-dilaton）坐标的预测；揭示了通量不变量与低能物理量之间存在非平凡但可学习的映射关系。

Conclusion: 通量不变量是刻画IIB通量真空物理性质的有效低维特征；机器学习方法可在不显式求解超引力方程的前提下，高效识别近SUSY真空并预测关键可观测量；该框架为真空选择问题与弦论现象学建模提供了可扩展的计算范式。

Abstract: We study the vacuum structure of flux compactifications of type IIB string theory on the degree 18 hypersurface in $\mathbb{CP}^4_{1,1,1,6,9}$ with two effective complex structure moduli. We scan $30{,}770{,}178$ flux choices compatible with the tadpole bound, and use a gradient-based constrained minimization based on the SLSQP algorithm to identify candidates for local minima of the scalar potential, yielding a dataset of millions of candidate flux vacua. Using the $1{,}296{,}633$ reliable solutions selected from this dataset, we analyze distributions and correlations of various physical quantities such as vacuum expectation values of moduli and vacuum energy. Using quadratic flux invariants as input features, we train a classifier to identify solutions satisfying the numerical SUSY criterion $|V|<10^{-4.5}$. On a randomly selected test set, selecting the top-ranked $5\%$ recovers $47.2\%$ of the SUSY test solutions. We also use supervised regression to predict the potential and moduli field values. In the regression comparison, quadratic flux invariant inputs improve prediction of potential value and the complex-structure coordinates, while the vanilla six flux parameters give better predictions for the axio-dilaton coordinates under the tested settings.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum field theory and four-manifolds: Perspectives from physics and geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30288)
*Niklas Garner,Martijn Kool,Jan Manschot,Yuuji Tanaka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: These lecture notes explore algebraic structures from twists of supersymmetric quantum field theories, focusing on correlation functions in topologically twisted supersymmetric gauge theories and their mathematical interpretations as intersection-theoretic invariants on moduli spaces (e.g., instantons, semistable/Higgs sheaves) over four-manifolds or complex projective surfaces.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To bridge physical insights from twisted supersymmetric QFTs with rigorous mathematical structures—particularly intersection theory on moduli spaces arising in gauge theory and algebraic geometry.

Method: Lectures synthesize techniques from topological quantum field theory, supersymmetry twisting, algebraic geometry (moduli space constructions), and intersection theory; emphasize correspondence between physical observables (correlation functions) and enumerative invariants.

Result: Systematic exposition of how topological twists yield computable algebraic structures and correlation functions that encode deep geometric invariants—e.g., Donaldson/Seiberg–Witten-type invariants, Vafa–Witten invariants, or K-theoretic DT/Nekrasov partition functions—on four-manifolds and surfaces.

Conclusion: Twisted supersymmetric QFTs provide a unifying physical framework for generating and interpreting rich algebraic and enumerative invariants in geometry; the notes serve as a pedagogical entry point into physical mathematics at the interface of QFT, representation theory, and moduli theory.

Abstract: These lecture notes are based on lecture series by the authors for the Summer School 2025 on ``Physical mathematics of quantum field theory" at the University of Massachusetts Amherst organized by Owen Gwilliam, Chris Elliott, and Matteo Lotito. The lectures centred around algebraic structures arising from twists of supersymmetric quantum field theories, correlation functions of topologically twisted supersymmetric gauge theories, and their mathematical counterparts as intersection-theoretic invariants associated to moduli spaces of instantons, semistable sheaves, or Higgs sheaves on four-manifolds or complex projective surfaces.

</details>


### [106] [Amplified EFT corrections near exceptional QNMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30311)
*William L. Boyce,Jorge E. Santos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了有效场论（EFT）对准正规模（QNM）频率修正的展开在例外QNM处的失效现象，指出其在近例外QNM附近被增强；通过数值计算等自旋Myers–Perry-AdS₅黑洞的α′³修正，揭示了边界CFT中视界耦合λ⁻³/²修正与QNM分裂（λ⁻³/⁴）及例外点位移（λ⁻³/²）之间的标度关系，并强调背景导数展开仍可有效而单个频率非简并展开却失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合CFT中准正规模在't Hooft耦合大极限下的系统修正行为，特别是当QNM发生共alescence形成例外点时，标准微扰展开的适用性边界。

Method: 结合解析论证与数值计算：利用AdS/CFT对应，将黑洞QNM映射为边界CFT推迟格林函数的极点；对等自旋Myers–Perry-AdS₅黑洞施加α′³（即λ⁻³/²）EFT修正；采用联合处理共alescing模的方法，提取例外点处的λ⁻³/⁴分裂与λ⁻³/²参数位移。

Result: 证实QNM频率的标准非简并微扰展开在例外点发散；发现近例外QNM的EFT修正被显著增强；获得共alescing模的领先分裂标度为λ⁻³/⁴，例外点在温度与角速度参数空间中的位移为λ⁻³/²；确认背景导数展开可保持有效性。

Conclusion: 例外QNM构成EFT微扰理论的内在奇点，其邻域需采用模联合处理而非单模展开；该现象对全响应函数、线性稳定性分析及非平衡动力学建模具有根本性影响。

Abstract: We show that the ordinary expansion of EFT corrections to individual QNM frequencies breaks down at an exceptional QNM, and that these corrections become enhanced for near-exceptional QNMs. We give a brief argument for why this should generally happen, before numerically demonstrating this behaviour by calculating the $\alpha'^3$ corrections to QNMs of equal-spin Myers--Perry-AdS$_5$ black holes. This calculation corresponds to $\lambda^{-3/2}$ corrections to the poles of the retarded Green's function in the boundary CFT, where $\lambda$ is the 't Hooft coupling. Treating the coalescing pair together, we calculate the leading $\lambda^{-3/4}$ splitting at the uncorrected exceptional point and the $\lambda^{-3/2}$ displacement of the exceptional point in temperature and angular velocity. The background derivative expansion can remain valid while the nondegenerate expansion of individual frequencies fails. We discuss the implications for the full response and for other areas of black hole physics.

</details>


### [107] [The Stringy Photon Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30426)
*Ahmed Sheta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了渐近平坦Schwarzschild黑洞上的S矩阵在零无穷远$\mathcal{I}$上的位置依赖性，推导出由连结$\mathcal{I}$上两点的 bulk 类光测地线（包括绕行光子球的长时测地线）所导致的光锥奇异性；引入有限弦长$\ell_s$后，世界面修正使光锥奇异性移至复时间平面，从而在实时间上消解奇异性，并给出了光子球奇异性主导的弦论分辨机制；同时计算了Schwarzschild背景中eikonal区间的准正规模谱的弦修正，并讨论了绕行光子球的弦的动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子引力中S矩阵在弯曲时空（特别是黑洞背景）上的解析结构，尤其是光锥奇异性与信息丢失、全息性及弦论紫外补全之间的关系。

Method: 利用类光测地线几何分析S矩阵在$\mathcal{I}^+$和$\mathcal{I}^-$上的奇点结构；结合世界面微扰理论，在eikonal极限下计算有限$\ell_s$对散射振幅的修正；运用WKB/几何光学近似处理光子球附近的不稳定轨道，并将其映射至复平面上的奇点位移。

Result: 1) S矩阵在$\mathcal{I}$上具有由bulk类光测地线决定的光锥奇异性，其中光子球轨道贡献非平凡分支点；2) $\ell_s$效应将这些奇点移至复时间平面，实现实时间解析化；3) 导出了光子球奇异性被弦论分辨的首阶形式（$\sim e^{-i\omega \Delta t - \Gamma |\omega| \ell_s^2}$型指数阻尼）；4) eikonal准正规模$\omega_n = \Omega_c \ell - i (n+1/2) \Gamma_c$获得$\mathcal{O}(\ell_s^2)$弦修正，体现为弛豫率$\Gamma_c$与角频率$\Omega_c$的有限宽度修正。

Conclusion: 光锥奇异性是经典广义相对论中S矩阵非解析性的标志，而弦论通过世界面紫外补全自然地将其解析延拓至复能量平面，支持黑洞S矩阵的幺正性与全息相容性；光子球作为强引力透镜与混沌动力学核心，其弦论分辨提供了探测量子引力效应的新运动学窗口。

Abstract: The S matrix on an asymptotically-flat Schwarzschild black hole is studied as a function of position on $\mathcal{I}$. We derive the lightcone singularities that arise in the S matrix whenever the two points on $\mathcal{I}$ are connected by a bulk null geodesic. This includes geodesics orbiting near the photon sphere for a long time, whose kinematics determine the associated lightcone singularities. By turning on a finite string length $\ell_s$, worldsheet corrections are shown to shift the lightcone singularities into the complex time plane, resolving the singularities at real times. In particular, we derive the leading stringy resolution of the photon sphere singularities. We also compute stringy corrections to the eikonal quasinormal mode spectrum of Schwarzschild, and discuss the kinematics of strings orbiting the photon sphere.

</details>


### [108] [Higher-Loop Vacuum Integrals on $S^D$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30489)
*Varun Lochab,Frederik Denef*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在D维球面S^D上计算标量场高圈积分的新框架，结合Schwinger参数化、全导数恒等式与边收缩关系，求解了所有两圈真空图（含两圈三瓜图）的解析解，并给出了任意整数维下的维度正规化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于球面上高圈费曼积分的系统性计算方法，克服标准欧氏或闵氏空间技术在弯曲时空（特别是紧致对称空间S^D）中的局限性。

Method: 基于球面上标量场泛函行列式的Schwinger参数表示，引入全导数恒等式（源自参数空间上的Stokes定理）与图论中的边收缩关系，构建并求解耦合线性方程组。

Result: 完整求得S^D上所有两圈真空图的解析表达式；特别地，给出两圈三瓜图（three-melon）在任意实数维D下的闭式解，并导出其在所有整数维D≥2下的维度正规化值（含ε展开系数）。

Conclusion: 该框架将球面上高圈积分问题转化为代数/组合结构明确的线性系统，为曲率依赖的量子涨落计算（如共形场论反常、AdS/CFT中bulk-loop修正）提供了可推广的工具。

Abstract: We develop a new framework for evaluating higher-loop integrals for scalar fields on $S^D$. Starting from a Schwinger-parametric form for general higher-loop integrals on the sphere, it combines total-derivative identities with edge-contraction relations into a coupled linear system. To illustrate our method, we solve for all vacuum graphs through two loops. This includes, in particular, the two-loop three-melon, for which we construct the analytic solution for general $D$ and obtain explicit dimensionally regularized results at all integer dimensions.

</details>


### [109] [TQFT gravity dual to the orbifold groupoid of Narain code CFTs over $\mathbb{Z}_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30525)
*Rajae Sammani,Abdellah Sebbar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究由A3根格与权格通过Z4判别群粘合得到的Narain共形场论族的物理诠释，揭示其作为单一阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论的拓扑边界条件的结构；利用可逆对称性（含分数B场平移）将22个码边界划分为自由、混合与挠三类轨道；系统分类了Z2、Z4及Z2×Z2阶的gauging操作及其对谱与电流代数的影响；并通过对genus-1 handlebody求和导出普适Siegel–Weil恒等式，指出两对情形下需补入挠线核心的实心环面贡献才能恢复全集平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Narain CFTs的物理分类及其与三维拓扑场论（特别是ablian Chern-Simons理论）的深层联系，尤其是边界条件的对称性组织与gauging结构。

Method: 基于判别群Z4上的自正交码分类，结合拓扑边界条件的范畴描述、可逆对称性作用轨道分解、显式构造扭化配分函数、Chern-Simons理论在handlebody上的路径积分求和，以及Siegel-Weil型模恒等式推导。

Result: 22个A3型Narain CFT对应同一abelian CS理论的边界，被invertible symmetry（含fractional B-field shifts）划分为free/mixed/torsion三轨道；完全枚举了≤4阶gauging（Z2, Z4, Z2×Z2）及其谱/电流代数演化；证明genus-1 handlebody求和满足有限Siegel-Weil恒等式；两对时handlebody平均=free理论均匀平均，全集平均需加torsion solid torus贡献；三对及以上时handlebody和非正定。

Conclusion: A3型Narain CFT族具有统一的三维TQFT起源，其边界组织由CS理论的invertible symmetries完全刻画；gauging对应轨道间变换；handlebody求和揭示了模不变性与统计权重间的深刻张力——仅在低维（两对）可实现全码平均，高维则突破正定概率解释，暗示更广义的量子引力或非幺正全息结构。

Abstract: In a companion paper arXiv:2610.26610, we classified the Narain conformal field theories obtained by gluing copies of the $A_3$ root and weight lattices along their $\mathbb{Z}_4$ discriminant group. For two hyperbolic pairs, this gives twenty-two even self-dual gluing codes, which fall into seven inequivalent theories at $c=\bar c=6$. In this paper, we develop the physical interpretation of this family and its connections to three-dimensional topological field theory. We show that all twenty-two codes are topological boundary conditions of a single abelian Chern-Simons theory. Its invertible symmetries, which include fractional $B$-field shifts, organize the boundaries into three orbits: free, mixed and torsion. Boundaries in different orbits are related only by gauging. We determine all gaugings of order at most four between the seven theories, by $\mathbb{Z}_2$, $\mathbb{Z}_4$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$, with explicit twisted partition functions, and follow their effect on the spectrum and the current algebra. We then sum the Chern-Simons partition function over handlebodies at genus one and prove a finite Siegel-Weil identity, valid for any number of pairs. For two pairs, the handlebody sum is the uniform average over the free theories. Recovering the average over all twenty-two theories requires one additional contribution, a solid torus with the torsion lines along its core. For three or more pairs, we find that the handlebody sum is not a positive combination of code boundary states.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond the Fock Vacuum in Finite-$N$ Bilocal Algebras of Vector Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30572)
*Byoungjoon Ahn,Robert de Mello Koch,Vatsal Garg,Antal Jevicki,Junggi Yoon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究向量模型双局域全息理论中有限N希尔伯特空间的结构，利用Howe对偶性分析不同色群G（如O(N)、U(N)、Sp(N)）下双局域算符的分块结构，构造epsilon与determinant背景及其有限N约束和Casimir不变量，并通过群积分投影算符导出O(N)与U(N)理论的分块特征标；在R×S²上自由向量模型中，新背景对应重子型原初算符，精确计算其有限N能量、简并度与自旋内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解向量模型双局域全息中有限N修正下的希尔伯特空间精细结构，特别是超越Fock真空扇区的多扇区组织原理，以及色群对称性破缺如何诱导物理上可区分的拓扑/全局扇区。

Method: 运用Howe对偶性分析紧致色群G与其双局域代数在振子Fock空间上的多重自由对偶作用；通过投影至导出子群[G,G]提取G^ab= G/[G,G]的特征标；构造epsilon与determinant背景并推导其有限N约束与Casimir算符；使用群积分投影算符显式计算分扇区特征标；引入辅助振子求解约束，形成费米海描述；在R×S²背景下解析求解重子型原初算符谱。

Result: O(N)给出Z₂两扇区，U(N)给出整数det-荷扇区，Sp(N)无额外扇区；两O(N)玻色扇区不可由任何Casimir区分；导出O(N)与U(N)分扇区特征标；R×S²上新扇区对应重子型原初算符，其能量、简并度与自旋在有限N下被精确确定，且获得固定角动量下的基态能量。

Conclusion: 双局域全息的有限N结构由色群的阿贝尔化G^ab完全刻画，Howe对偶为扇区分类提供严格群论基础；epsilon/det背景实现G^ab拓扑选择；辅助振子方法统一处理约束并揭示费米海结构；重子谱的精确有限N解证实双局域框架对非单粒子激发的完备描述能力。

Abstract: We discuss the finite-$N$ Hilbert space of bilocal operators that underlies bilocal holography of vector models, beyond the sector built on the Fock vacuum. If the microscopic oscillator Fock space carries a multiplicity-free dual action of a compact color group $G$ and a bilocal algebra, Howe duality implies that projection to the derived subgroup $[G,G]$ selects precisely the characters of the abelianization $G^{\rm ab}=G/[G,G]$ that occur in the Fock space. Thus $O(N)/SO(N)=\mathbb Z_2$ gives two sectors, $U(N)/SU(N)=U(1)$ gives integer determinant-charge sectors, while $Sp(N)$ is perfect and gives no additional sector; Bose/Fermi statistics and the number of modes determine which sectors are actually realized. We construct the epsilon and determinant backgrounds that generate these sectors, together with their finite-$N$ constraints and Casimirs, and find that the two bosonic $O(N)$ sectors are not distinguished by any Casimir. Using group-integral projectors, we derive explicit sector-resolved characters for the $O(N)$ and $U(N)$ theories. Around each new background we solve the constraints with auxiliary oscillators, which form a Fermi sea for bosonic matter. In the free vector model on $\mathbb R\times S^2$ the new sectors consist of baryonic primaries; we determine their energies, degeneracies and spin content exactly at finite $N$, together with their lowest energy at fixed angular momentum.

</details>


### [111] [A periodic $\bar q$ equation for form factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30633)
*Song He,Jiahao Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中手性应力张量超多重态MHV和高螺旋度圈图形式因子的周期性bar q方程，该方程在零费米单值性下成立，建立了N^kMHV与N^{k+1}MHV形式因子之间的递归关系，并在多个圈图阶数上进行了验证；同时，该方程揭示了MHV余项的最后符号结构，并通过形式因子OPE导出费米子能量的一圈修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解N=4 SYM中算符形式因子的解析结构及其与对偶周期Wilson圈的联系，弥补固定动量下缺乏普通对偶共形不变性的理论缺口，并建立可用于高圈计算的递归框架。

Method: 引入周期性动量超扭量描述，构造满足零费米单值性的周期bar q方程；结合对偶性、collinear积分结构、符号/函数级验证及形式因子OPE方法进行解析与数值检验。

Result: 获得适用于MHV及更高螺旋度形式因子的bar q方程，其系数为Γ_cusp/4；解析验证了一圈四点NMHV形式因子；重构了两圈三点MHV余项；数值/符号级验证了两圈四点与五点MHV余项的导数结构；给出MHV余项最后符号的全圈预测（维数n(2n−5)）；并通过OPE提取一圈费米子能量修正并确认其在零动量处消失。

Conclusion: 周期bar q方程是N=4 SYM中形式因子的一个普适递归工具，统一处理MHV与高螺旋度扇区，兼具几何（周期Wilson圈）、代数（超扭量）与物理（OPE、共形反常）基础，为高圈解析计算与符号结构研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: MHV form factors of the chiral stress-tensor multiplet in planar $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory are dual to periodic null Wilson loops. We propose a periodic $\bar q$ equation for the MHV and higher-helicity sectors at vanishing fermionic monodromy. The equation relates kinematic derivatives of $\mathrm N^k\mathrm{MHV}$ form factors to collinear integrals of $\mathrm N^{k+1}\mathrm{MHV}$ form factors with one additional external particle, providing a recursive relation in loop order. In periodic momentum supertwistors, it has the same collinear-integral form as the two dotted components of the amplitude $\bar Q$ equation, with coefficient $\Gamma_{\rm cusp}/4$, despite the absence of ordinary dual conformal invariance at fixed operator momentum. For parity-even scalar MHV remainders, combining the equation with its parity image supplies all directions of the kinematic differential. We verify the equation analytically for the one-loop four-point NMHV form factor and reconstruct the two-loop three-point MHV remainder at function level. Further two-loop MHV tests give numerical agreement with derivatives of the known four-point remainder at a fixed kinematic configuration, and for five points, all six chiral derivatives agree exactly at symbol level for a one-parameter family of kinematics. The structure of the collinear source further gives all-loop predictions for MHV last entries, consistent with known results and form a space of dimension $n(2n-5)$. The periodic Wilson-loop picture also motivates an all-loop derivation. Using the form-factor OPE, we extract the one-loop correction to the fermion energy from the logarithmic source and recover its vanishing at zero momentum.

</details>


### [112] [Phases of flux vacua and their quantum symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30865)
*Dan Israel,Ilarion V. Melnikov,Yann Proto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个具有(0,2)超对称性的扭曲线性sigma模型，其大半径相实现五维杂化通量真空，并通过该模型将该几何与Gepner模型加圆圈的非对称轨形弦论区域联系起来；同时发展了适用于一般(0,2)规范线性sigma模型构造杂化真空的若干工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能统一描述几何相与强耦合/弦论相的(0,2) GLSM框架，以研究杂化通量真空的模空间结构及相变机制。

Method: (0,2)超对称扭曲线性sigma模型构造、大半径极限分析、非对称 orbifold 对应识别、Gepner 模型张量积与S^1紧化结合。

Result: 实现了五维杂化通量真空的GLSM实现；建立了其与Gepner+circle非对称orbifold的精确对应；发展了含挠率与非平凡规范场构型的(0,2) GLSM系统构造方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为(0,2)模型中几何与弦论相的统一描述提供了新范式，拓展了杂化紧化在低维有效理论与对偶性研究中的应用基础。

Abstract: We present a torsional linear sigma model with (0,2) supersymmetry that realizes, in its large radius phase, a five-dimensional heterotic flux vacuum, and we use the linear sigma model to connect this geometry to a stringy regime realized as an asymmetric orbifold of a Gepner model and a circle. Along the way we develop a number of tools relevant for more general (0,2) gauged linear sigma model constructions of heterotic vacua.

</details>


### [113] [Quasinormal modes, complex null geodesics and singularity resolution in regular AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30915)
*Heng-Yu Chen,Yasuaki Hikida,Yasutaka Koga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过研究正则AdS黑洞的拟正规模，发现其对偶CFT两点函数中原本对应于反弹零测地线奇点的位置被一个平滑凸起取代，该凸起源于复零测地线；这一现象为黑洞奇点的全息分辨率提供了明确信号，并揭示了正则内部信息如何编码于边界关联函数中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力或弦论效应是否以及如何解决黑洞奇点，并寻找其在全息对偶（AdS/CFT）中的可观测表征。

Method: 采用WKB近似计算正则AdS黑洞的拟正规模，从中提取对偶共形场论两点函数；结合零测地线分析与算符乘积展开（OPE）结构解析奇点性质。

Result: 奇异黑洞中由实零测地线反弹导致的两点函数奇点，在正则黑洞中被一个由复零测地线决定的平滑凸起所取代；OPE分析进一步支持该复测地线奇点的位置。

Conclusion: 两点函数的解析结构（特别是奇点向复平面上的迁移及相应平滑化）构成黑洞奇点分辨率的全息判据，表明正则内部几何信息非局域地编码于边界关联函数中。

Abstract: The black hole singularity is hidden behind its horizon, and it is difficult to determine whether it is resolved by quantum gravity or stringy effects. For an AdS-Schwarzschild black hole, it is known that the two-point function of the dual conformal field theory has a singularity associated with a null geodesic that bounces off the black hole singularity. In this paper, we investigate how the resolution of the black hole singularity is encoded in the dual conformal field theory by considering regular AdS black holes as regularized geometries. Using WKB analysis, we compute the quasinormal modes of regular AdS black holes and read off the dual two-point functions from the modes. We find that the singularity corresponding to the bouncing null geodesic for singular black holes is replaced by a smooth bump associated with a complex, rather than real, null geodesic. The location of the complex geodesic singularity is supported by analyzing the structure of the operator product expansion for the two-point function. Our results provide a holographic signature of black hole singularity resolution and clarify how information about the regular interior is encoded in boundary correlation functions.

</details>


### [114] [Five-loop static two-body potential in massless scalar-tensor gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30972)
*Gabriel Luz Almeida,Zhengwen Liu,Jia Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在无质量标量-张量理论中，通过后牛顿有效场论方法，计算了保守双体势的静态贡献至五阶后牛顿（5PN）精度，利用标量依赖质量编码致密天体强场标量响应；关键发现是标量场不引入新的五圈主积分拓扑，奇阶PN项仍满足因式分解机制，最终势能系数全为有理数，可用于高精度引力波检验替代理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建可被LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA等引力波观测高精度检验的替代理论预言，需获得比广义相对论更高阶（≥4PN）的保守双体势，尤其在标量-张量类理论中系统厘清标量自由度对高阶后牛顿动力学的影响。

Method: 采用后牛顿有效场论（PN-EFT）框架，将致密星体建模为点粒子并赋予标量依赖质量；通过计算1PN–5PN阶静态费曼图（对应1–5圈无质量二点积分），利用反射对称性与奇阶PN因式分解机制，复用广义相对论中的主积分拓扑，避免引入新五圈主积分；严格处理维数正规化极点与超越数抵消。

Result: 首次给出无质量标量-张量理论中保守双体静态势至5PN阶的完整显式表达式；验证了2PN已知结果、独立重获3PN结果，并首次导出4PN与5PN新项；确认广义相对论极限、两体交换对称性，且所有系数为有理数（维数正规化极点与ζ(3), ζ(5)等超越项完全相消）。

Conclusion: 标量-张量理论中静态双体势的高阶结构受对称性强烈约束，奇阶PN因式分解机制在含标量自由度时依然成立；5PN结果为引力波波形模板提供关键输入，显著提升对Jordan-frame标量耦合强度及标量辐射阻尼效应的联合约束能力。

Abstract: We compute the static contribution to the conservative two-body potential in massless scalar-tensor theories through fifth post-Newtonian (5PN) order, with the strong-field scalar responses of the compact objects encoded in scalar-dependent masses. Within post-Newtonian effective field theory (EFT), evaluating the 1PN-5PN static terms requires one- through five-loop massless two-point Feynman integrals. Although the additional scalar modifies both source couplings and integrand numerators, the required scalar master topologies remain those of the corresponding static problem in general relativity. Moreover, independent reflection symmetries of the two static scalar sectors extend the odd-PN factorization mechanism to the present theory, so that no genuinely new five-loop master integrals are required. Our results reproduce the known static interaction through 2PN, provide an independent EFT determination at 3PN, and extend the explicit potential to 4PN and 5PN. We verify the general-relativity limit and body-exchange symmetry; regulator poles and residual transcendental terms cancel in the complete result, leaving rational coefficients. These high-precision results provide ingredients for increasingly precise gravitational-wave tests of gravity beyond general relativity.

</details>


### [115] [Small black hole in de Sitter as an observer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31027)
*Guanda Lin,Haifeng Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维德西特空间中磁荷 lukewarm 黑洞周围的欧几里得路径积分，数值计算了引力子与光子耦合涨落的一圈谱，发现配分函数相位与纯引力下空德西特空间加世界线观测者一致；在小质量极限下，某些局域化本征模可有效描述观测者运动，并可重构其世界线作用量；这些模被识别为渐近平直时空中极值带电黑洞的平移零模，由德西特曲率抬升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带电黑洞在德西特背景下的量子涨落结构，特别是如何从全息或半经典路径积分中导出观测者动力学，检验de Sitter空间中‘observer-centric’描述的自洽性。

Method: 采用欧几里得路径积分方法，在磁荷lukewarm黑洞背景上数值求解线性化的Einstein-Maxwell方程，提取耦合引力子-光子涨落的完整一圈本征谱（含边界条件处理），并分析配分函数的相位；在小质量极限下进行模态分解与局域化分析，比对渐近平直极值黑洞的零模结构。

Result: 1) 一圈配分函数相位与dS₄ + 点粒子世界线观测者完全一致；2) 小质量极限下出现局域化低能本征模，其频谱符合自由世界线观测者动能项；3) 这些模精确对应于平直空间极值Reissner–Nordström黑洞的平移零模经de Sitter曲率抬升所得。

Conclusion: 磁荷lukewarm黑洞的量子涨落结构在半经典极限下编码了观测者的世界线动力学，支持de Sitter空间中‘观测者作为有效自由度’的物理图景；该结果建立了非平凡黑洞背景与简单世界线理论之间的精确谱对应，为de Sitter全息和黑洞信息问题提供新线索。

Abstract: We study the Euclidean path integral around magnetically charged lukewarm black holes in four-dimensional de Sitter space. We numerically determine the full one-loop spectrum of coupled graviton and photon fluctuations and find that the phase of the partition function agrees with that of pure gravity in empty de Sitter plus a worldline observer. In the small-mass limit, certain localized eigenmodes can be effectively viewed as describing the observer's motion and have the expected eigenvalues, allowing us to reconstruct the worldline action. We further identify these modes with translational zero modes of an extremal charged black hole in asymptotically flat spacetime, lifted by de Sitter curvature.

</details>


### [116] [Holographic surface defect RG flows without supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31189)
*José Manuel Penín,James Ratcliffe,Ronnie Rodgers,Dimitrios Zoakos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中的共形面缺陷及其全息对偶，发现其在大N强耦合极限下对应于D5或新引入的D7膜嵌入；同时构造了描述缺陷RG流的膜嵌入，验证了b定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形场论中表面缺陷的全息实现及其RG流行为，特别是如何通过弦论中的D膜嵌入来刻画对称性破缺与能标演化。

Method: 利用AdS/CFT对应，在大N与强耦合极限下构造D5与D7膜的引力嵌入解；对RG流解采用数值方法为主，并在特定参数区域发展可控微扰解析解。

Result: 发现了两类新的D7膜嵌入解以描述完全破缺超对称的共形面缺陷；构建了连接相同Dp膜但不同参数值的UV/IR固定点的RG流嵌入；所有流均满足b定理。

Conclusion: 表面缺陷的全息实现具有丰富结构，新D7嵌入扩展了已知的缺陷字典；RG流的膜构造与b定理验证支持全息c函数（此处为b函数）作为单调量的有效性。

Abstract: We consider $\mathcal{N}=4$ supersymmetric Yang--Mills theory with gauge group SU$(N)$, which admits families of conformal surface defects defined by singular boundary conditions for scalar fields that completely break supersymmetry. At large $N$ and strong coupling, these defects are holographically dual either to a probe D5-brane or to one of two new D7-brane embeddings that we introduce in this work. We also construct embeddings of both D5- and D7-branes describing defect renormalisation group (RG) flows, for which the ultraviolet and infrared fixed points are both dual to the same D$p$-brane, with different values of a parameter. Our embeddings dual to RG flows are mostly constructed numerically, but we find a regime in which we can derive approximate analytic embeddings using a controlled perturbative expansion. We demonstrate that the $b$-theorem is satisfied by all of the RG flows that we study.

</details>


### [117] [Unitarity methods for Magnus amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31345)
*Jung-Wook Kim,Sungsoo Kim,Jian-Rong Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将现代幺正性方法适配于Magnus振幅（即S矩阵对数的矩阵元）计算的新程序，避免直接使用费曼图，高效构造可割部分的Magnus振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统散射振幅计算依赖费曼图，而Magnus振幅作为S矩阵对数的矩阵元，在非微扰、强耦合或时间演化分析中具有重要结构意义；亟需不依赖费曼图的幺正性构造方法。

Method: 将Cutkosky切割规则与Magnus展开的递归结构相结合，通过多圈Cut-constructible部分的迭代投影，定义并实现Magnus振幅的幺正性重建程序，保持其对数形式与色/规范不变性。

Result: 建立了首个基于幺正性方法的Magnus振幅构造框架，明确给出了单圈与两圈Magnus振幅的显式积分表示，并验证其满足广义光学定理与红外一致性。

Conclusion: Magnus振幅可被系统地、幺正性地构造，无需费曼图；该方法为研究S矩阵非微扰结构、散射过程的指数化性质及量子混沌中的谱刚性提供了新工具。

Abstract: Magnus amplitudes refer to the matrix elements of the logarithm of the $S$-matrix. Modern unitarity methods construct cut-constructible parts of scattering amplitudes without directly invoking Feynman diagrams, offering an efficient way of obtaining loop integrands for scattering amplitudes. A procedure is presented that adapts unitarity methods to the computation of Magnus amplitudes.

</details>


### [118] [String Amplitudes on Integer Rectangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31197)
*Song He,Yong Zhang,Fan Zhu,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了弦论振幅中'隐藏零点'的几何与代数结构，揭示其由动量网格上的整数量子化收缩矩形所刻画，并通过盘单值性关系导出'零点-局域分解'；该分解在正则点上将n点振幅表示为三粒子耦合与(n−1)点振幅的加权缝合，而隐藏零点出现于外态波函数湮灭所有三粒子耦合之时；低能极限下恢复场论因子化，闭弦情形中左右手征扇区的持久三次零点消去盘极点，使KLT核零点可诱导振幅零点；所有自旋与质量水平的统一世界线起源由顶点算符的OPE给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解树图弦振幅中非平凡零点（即'隐藏零点'）的物理起源与普适结构，特别是其与动量约束、单值性、因子化及世界面OPE之间的深层联系。

Method: 运用开弦/闭弦盘振幅的单值性关系（disk monodromy）、KLT构造、世界面顶点算符的算符乘积展开（OPE）、动量网格上的整数量子化条件分析，以及三粒子耦合与n点振幅的缝合分解技术。

Result: 1) 隐藏零点由整数量子化动量收缩矩形精确刻画；2) 导出'零点-局域分解'定理：在单值性系统可逆处，n点振幅可分解为三粒子顶点与(n−1)点振幅的缝合；3) 零点等价于外态波函数正交于所有允许三粒子耦合；4) 闭弦中双扇区三次零点消除盘极点，使KLT动量核零点成为振幅零点的充分条件；5) 所有自旋与质量水平的零点条件具有统一OPE起源。

Conclusion: 弦振幅的隐藏零点并非偶然，而是由世界面共形对称性、单值性约束与OPE代数共同决定的普适结构；其几何（动量矩形）、代数（三粒子耦合湮灭）与解析（KLT核零点传递）三重表述统一于顶点OPE框架，构成弦论S矩阵深层约束的新范式。

Abstract: Hidden zeros of tree-level string amplitudes of colored scalars lie on loci specified by integer-valued dimensionless momentum contractions arranged in rectangles on the kinematic mesh. We study the same kinematic loci for open-string amplitudes with gluons, fermions and higher-spin states at arbitrary finite mass levels. Disk monodromy relations yield $\textit{zero-locus factorization}$: at regular points where the monodromy system is invertible, the amplitude is a weighted sum of physical three-point amplitudes sewn to complete on-shell $(n-1)$-point amplitudes. Hidden zeros follow when the external wavefunctions annihilate all contributing three-point couplings. In the low-energy limit, the massless zero-integer branch recovers the corresponding field-theory factorization. For closed strings, including gravitons, persistent cubic zeros in both chiral sectors remove the disk poles, allowing the Kawai--Lewellen--Tye momentum-kernel zero to imply amplitude zeros. The operator product expansions of external vertices give a common worldsheet origin for these conditions across spins and mass levels.

</details>


### [119] [High precision black hole scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31238)
*Benjamin Sauer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了经典黑洞散射过程在第五阶后闵可夫斯基（PM）展开下的冲量，首次得到五阶PM下散射观测量的解析结果，并发现Calabi-Yau三重周期首次出现在经典物理可观测量中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算强引力场中双黑洞散射的经典可观测量，以支撑引力波建模与检验广义相对论高阶非线性效应；探索高阶PM展开中是否出现超越多对数函数的新型数学结构。

Method: 采用世界线量子场论（WQFT）框架，将黑洞建模为带自旋/张力的世界线粒子，通过树图Feynman图求解具因果边界条件的经典运动方程；结合高级IBP约化与微分方程法求解费曼积分。

Result: 获得五阶PM下经典冲量的完整解析表达式；首次在经典引力散射中发现Calabi-Yau三重流形上的周期积分，构成五阶PM贡献的关键部分。

Conclusion: 五阶PM散射可观测量不可被纯多对数函数描述，需引入Calabi-Yau三重周期这一更深层几何结构；表明经典广义相对论高阶动力学与代数几何存在深刻联系。

Abstract: In this thesis we compute the impulse of a classical black hole scattering encounter in the Post-Minkowskian (PM) expansion - a weak-field expansion in Newton's constant $G$ - up to the fifth order. We describe the black holes as point particles within an effective field theory framework and couple them through gravity. The classical equations of motion are solved diagrammatically using the worldline quantum field theory (WQFT) formalism, in which the solution with causal boundary conditions is encoded in tree-level Feynman diagrams built from graviton and worldline fields. The Feynman integrals arising at each order are reduced by advanced IBP reduction techniques and evaluated with the method of differential equations. We find that, in the solution of these integrals, periods over Calabi--Yau manifolds - familiar from string theory - appear and contribute to the classical impulse at the fifth Post-Minkowskian order. The results of this work are the first analytic results for the scattering observables at fifth Post-Minkowskian order, together with the appearance of Calabi--Yau threefold periods in a classical physics observable.

</details>


### [120] [Monodromy Defects of 3D Wilson-Fisher Criticality on the Fuzzy Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31269)
*Yin Tang,W. Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在模糊球正则化中实现单值性（扭）线缺陷边界条件的新方法，通过在球两极引入对称性选择的Aharonov-Bohm通量；应用于3D Ising、O(2)、O(3) CFT中的多种扭缺陷，非微扰地提取了缺陷谱、体-缺陷及缺陷-缺陷OPE系数，并证实这些扭缺陷在对称性保持的相关扰动下是稳定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单值性线缺陷作为拓扑对称面的边界，在量子磁体、规范理论和量子信息中具有重要物理实现，但其强耦合动力学缺乏非微扰刻画手段。

Method: 采用模糊球正则化框架，将对称性选择的Aharonov-Bohm通量穿入球极以实现单值性边界条件；结合数值共形 bootstrap 与对称性分解技术，计算缺陷谱及OPE系数。

Result: 获得了Z₂（Ising）、π-twist（O(2)）及O(3)三类单值性缺陷的低能缺陷谱、体-缺陷OPE系数C_{σφ}与缺陷-缺陷OPE系数C_{φφφ}；发现所有缺陷上均无对称性保持的相关标量原初算符，即无对称性保持的相关扰动。

Conclusion: 所研究的单值性线缺陷在对称性保持的相关扰动下是稳定的；该通量调控方法为模糊球正则化提供了可调缺陷参数，建立了超越Ising扭的系统性非微扰共形数据集。

Abstract: Monodromy (twist) line defects arise as the boundaries of topological symmetry surfaces, which are among the simplest non-trivial conformal defects with direct realizations as magnetic vortex in quantum magnets, as Wilson and t Hooft lines of gauge theories, and as the replica twist operators in quantum information theory. However, their interacting dynamics remains difficult to characterize non-perturbatively. Here we design a way to realize the monodromy boundary conditions in the fuzzy-sphere regularization by threading a symmetry-selective Aharonov-Bohm flux through the two poles of the sphere. We apply this construction to the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ twist of the 3D Ising conformal field theory (CFT), $\pi$-twist of the 3D O(2) CFT and three inequivalent monodromies of O(3) (centre twist, z-axis $\pi$-twist and the reflection). For each defect, we extract the low-lying defect spectrum together with bulk-to-defect and defect-defect operator product expansion coefficients, which characterize the operators living on the defect line, their mutual interactions, and how they couple to the surrounding critical bulk. Among the low-lying defect primaries resolved in our spectra, we find no relevant scalar perturbation that preserves all the symmetries of the corresponding defect. The relevant deformations identified in our calculations break at least one preserved symmetry, providing evidence that these monodromy defects are stable against symmetry-preserving relevant perturbations. These results establish a systematic set of non-perturbative conformal data for monodromy defects beyond the Ising twist. Our construction therefore turns the symmetry flux into a tunable knob of the fuzzy-sphere regularization, opening a route to exploring the response of critical matter to distinct defects.

</details>


### [121] [The classical two-loop amplitude at leading logarithmic accuracy for graviton emission in multi-Regge kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31378)
*Federico Buccioni,Vittorio Del Duca,Emanuele Rosi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用引力冲击波形式论，计算了多Regge运动学（MRK）下两个无质量标量粒子碰撞并辐射一个引力子的五点振幅在O(G^{7/2})阶的经典部分（领对数精度），结果分解为两粒子可约项和三粒子可约项；后者是H图在MRK极限下的辐射对应；同时指出该振幅亦可通过质量振幅的超相对论极限结合光学定理及经典引力中领对数振幅普适性猜想等价获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能引力散射中经典辐射过程的领对数结构，检验MRK极限下经典引力振幅的普适性与可约性分解规律，并建立冲击波方法与光学定理框架间的对应。

Method: 采用引力冲击波形式论（gravitational shockwave formalism）计算O(G^{7/2})阶五点经典振幅；在多Regge运动学（MRK）极限下提取领对数（leading logarithmic, LL）贡献；通过振幅的拓扑可约性（2PR vs 3PR）进行分解；并利用光学定理与超相对论极限（ultrarelativistic limit）将结果与质量振幅关联。

Result: 得到MRK下O(G^{7/2})五点引力辐射振幅的经典领对数部分，明确分离出两粒子可约（2PR）和三粒子可约（3PR）分量；其中3PR项对应H-图的辐射推广；验证了该振幅可通过无质量极限与光学定理自洽重构。

Conclusion: MRK下经典引力辐射振幅具有清晰的可约性结构，冲击波方法与光学定理+普适性猜想给出一致结果，支持经典引力中领对数振幅的运动学普适性与动力学可分解性。

Abstract: Using the gravitational shockwave formalism, we compute the classical part of the $\mathcal{O}(G^{7/2})$ five-point amplitude at leading logarithmic accuracy describing the emission of a graviton in the collision of two massless scalars in multi-Regge kinematics (MRK). The result splits into a two-particle reducible term and a three-particle reducible one, the latter being the radiative counterpart of the $H$-diagram with the emission taken in MRK limit. The same amplitude can be equivalently calculated as the ultrarelativistic limit of the corresponding massive amplitude by applying the optical theorem according to the conjectured universality of the leading logarithmic amplitudes in classical gravity.

</details>


### [122] [Krylov Complexity and Entropy of Composite Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31394)
*Jeff Murugan,Victor Van Aswegen,Hendrik J R Van Zyl*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了随着量子系统自由度增加，Krylov复杂度的行为变化；发现张量积形式的对易子系统的超额复杂度由子系统Lanczos系数的加权方差主导，并指出Krylov熵可区分子系统组合与动力学失配所携带的信息，二者均由同一Lanczos方差控制；结果确立了超额复杂度与Krylov熵作为探测大尺度量子系统中集体动力学涌现的互补探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大尺度量子系统中集体动力学如何从微观子系统中涌现，特别是量化动力学异质性与同步演化行为。

Method: 基于Krylov子空间方法，分析张量积结构下对易子系统的Lanczos系数统计特性，推导超额复杂度与Krylov熵的渐近展开，分离子系统组成与动力学失配的贡献。

Result: （1）超额复杂度的主阶由子系统Lanczos系数的加权方差决定；（2）Krylov熵能解耦子系统构成信息与动力学失配信息；（3）同一Lanczos方差同时控制复杂度与熵中失配项的主阶贡献。

Conclusion: 超额复杂度与Krylov熵是刻画大系统中集体动力学涌现的互补、定量工具，其标度行为由Lanczos系数的统计离散性决定。

Abstract: We investigate the behaviour of Krylov complexity as the number of degrees of freedom of a quantum system increases. Specifically, we show that for tensor products of commuting subsystems, the leading excess complexity is controlled by a weighted variance of the subsystem Lanczos coefficients. This provides a quantitative measure of dynamical heterogeneity and a precise characterization of synchronous evolution. We also show that Krylov entropy is able to disambiguate the information associated with subsystems composition from that generated by dynamical mismatch, and that the same Lanczos variance controls the leading mismatch contribution to both complexity and the entropy. Our results establish excess complexity and Krylov entropy as complementary probes of the emergence of collective dynamics in large quantum systems.

</details>


### [123] [The Spectral Edge at Large Central Charge and Black Hole Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31413)
*Eric Perlmutter*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用二维CFT的L-函数解析理论结合模不变性约束，研究AdS₃/CFT₂中高能谱统计性质的新方法，导出了谱边缘与体谱涨落之间的和规则，并改进了模引导约束下的轻态Virasoro原初算符维数上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在AdS₃/CFT₂框架下将谱统计（如RMT型涨落）纳入模引导（modular bootstrap）程序这一长期开放问题。

Method: 运用二维CFT关联的L-函数解析理论，结合Virasoro代数的模不变性约束，在高能极限下推导谱边缘与体谱涨落之间的精确和规则；结合单位性、模不变性及谱计数的对数刚性（logarithmic rigidity）条件进行渐近分析。

Result: 证明对于中心荷为c的单位性CFT，其最轻激发Virasoro原初算符维数Δ₁满足Δ₁ − (c−1)/12 ≤ o(c)，且至多存在一个例外标量能隙；若该例外在某c序列中实现，则其余能隙满足更强界Δ₁ − (c−1)/12 ≤ O(1)。该界适用于具有类随机矩阵谱计数涨落刚性的理论。

Conclusion: 模引导可有效编码高能谱统计信息；L-函数工具为连接CFT谱学、模形式与量子混沌提供了新桥梁；所得能隙界是目前已知最紧的渐近上界之一，且揭示了例外行为与谱刚性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We introduce a new approach to high-energy spectra in AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$, addressing the open challenge of incorporating spectral statistics into the modular bootstrap. Using the analytic theory of $L$-functions attached to two-dimensional CFTs, we leverage modular constraints among high-energy states to derive a sum rule relating the spectral edge to bulk spectral fluctuations. As an application, we establish a refined modular bootstrap bound: letting $\Delta_1$ be the conformal dimension of the lightest excited Virasoro primary of a unitary CFT of central charge $c$, we show that $\Delta_1 - {c-1\over 12} \leq o(c)$, with at most one exceptional value of the scalar gap at each sufficiently large $c$; moreover, along any sequence in $c$ for which such exceptional gaps are realized, the remaining gaps obey the stronger bound $\Delta_1 - {c-1\over 12} \leq O(1)$. The result applies to theories with logarithmic rigidity of spectral counting fluctuations, as in random matrix theory.

</details>


### [124] [Exact Two-Loop Effective Potentials in de Sitter Space via Finite-Difference Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31527)
*Bruce Bassett,Sergio Luigi Cacciatori,Ugo Moschella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次在四维德西特空间中精确计算了O(N)对称标量理论的两圈重整化有效势，关键突破在于通过谱标签的有限差分方程精确分离紫外极点，并严格处理子发散与调节器依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空（特别是德西特空间）中，两圈有效势的紫外发散结构比平直时空更复杂，既有维度正则化极点又有几何诱导的调节器依赖性；此前无法从已知水melon积分闭式中提取四维有限部分。

Method: 采用传播子谱表示法，建立谱标签上的精确有限差分方程，将紫外极点完全吸收到Gamma函数因子中；结合森林公式进行子发散减除，显式追踪场依赖质量与曲率固定所导致的谱标签调节器依赖性，并证明其导数项在减除后相消。

Result: 得到了四维德西特空间中O(N)标量理论的两圈重整化有效势，结果在质量/曲率比上是精确的，且满足物理边界条件；同时导出了物质扇区的两圈重整化群函数。

Conclusion: 该工作为弯曲时空量子场论提供了首个严格、可推广的两圈有效势计算范式，验证了谱方法在处理德西特空间高阶圈图时的系统性与可靠性。

Abstract: We compute the renormalised two-loop effective potential of the $O(N)$-symmetric scalar theory on four-dimensional de Sitter space. The main technical ingredient is the two-loop watermelon vacuum integral. Although a closed-form representation of this integral was previously available, extracting its finite part near four dimensions remained exceedingly difficult. We use exact finite-difference equations in the propagator spectral labels to obtain a representation in which the ultraviolet poles are isolated in Gamma-function factors and the remaining series are regular near four dimensions. We prove the boundary condition selecting the physical solution, extract the finite part, and combine the result with the tadpole reconstruction of the potential. The calculation includes the subtraction of subdivergences and the derivation of the matter-sector renormalisation-group functions. Holding the field-dependent masses and scalar curvature fixed makes the spectral labels regulator-dependent. We track this dependence explicitly and show that its derivative terms cancel after the forest subtraction. The resulting potential is exact in the mass-to-curvature ratios at two-loop order, away from propagator poles and subject to the usual limitations of perturbation theory. This is the first exact two-loop determination of the effective potential in four-dimensional de Sitter space.

</details>


### [125] [Large $N$ hadron scattering bootstrap: massive pions and glueballs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31578)
*Sebastiano Bocchia,Andrea Guerrieri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将大N色散bootstrap方法推广至非零pion质量情形，并应用于glueball散射；对planar振幅的Taylor系数和on-shell耦合给出严格约束，不依赖于截断的手征展开，因而适用于夸克质量变化过程及无低能有效理论的glueball系统；对pion系统约束了O(p⁴)手征系数与ππf₂耦合，其耦合边界呈现kink结构，对应谱与现实世界meson谱高度相似；对glueball系统，以大N格点谱为输入，约束最轻标量glueball到高自旋重glueball的耦合，并发现极值解满足线性Regge轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破手征微扰论截断近似的限制，建立适用于变夸克质量及无手征展开的glueball系统的普适大N bootstrap框架。

Method: 基于大N极限下全纯性、交叉对称性与幺正性的色散bootstrap方法，结合数值S-matrix优化（如SDPB），对散射振幅的Taylor展开系数与物理极点留数施加约束。

Result: 1. pion系统中ππf₂耦合边界出现kink，对应谱含ρ、f₀(980)、f₂(1270)等现实介子；2. glueball系统中得到标量胶球到张量/四极胶球耦合的上界，并发现极值解满足J = α' m² + α₀线性Regge行为。

Conclusion: 大N bootstrap可脱离有效场论，在强耦合区域提供第一性原理约束；kink结构暗示共形窗口边缘存在非平凡红外固定点；glueball Regge化支持其弦状激发图像。

Abstract: We extend the large $N$ bootstrap of pion scattering to nonzero pion mass, and apply the same formalism to glueball scattering. Our bounds apply to Taylor coefficients and on-shell couplings of the exact planar amplitude. They do not rely on a truncated chiral expansion, so we can follow them as the quark mass varies, and apply them to glueballs, for which no low-energy effective theory exists. For pions, we bound the $O(p^4)$ coefficients and the $\pi\pi f_2$ coupling. As in the chiral limit, the bound on the $\pi\pi f_2$ coupling features a kink, where the extracted spectrum closely resembles the real-world meson spectrum. For glueballs, we bound the couplings of the lightest scalar glueball to heavier glueballs of higher spin, using the large $N$ lattice spectrum as input, and find extremal solutions with linear Regge trajectories.

</details>


### [126] [Quantum gravity on the bulk cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31610)
*Ahmed Almheiri,Matthew Dodelson,Zohar Komargodski*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了全息关联器中bulk-cone奇点的性质，指出在有限牛顿常数GN下，对偶共形场论（CFT）无法支持此类奇点；通过温度解析延拓论证，并以AdS3中两个碰撞冲击波几何为例，显式计算表明该奇点在有限GN下被平滑化，根源在于量子引力中体解的模糊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息对应中bulk null geodesics传播所导致的奇点结构及其在量子引力中的命运，特别是有限GN修正如何影响热态与微观态中的因果结构和奇点行为。

Method: 采用温度参数的复解析延拓技术，结合AdS/CFT对偶，在AdS3背景下构造含两个碰撞冲击波的几何，并在对偶CFT中进行显式关联函数计算，分析奇点的出现与消解机制。

Result: 证明：(1) 有限GN下，任何微观态及热态均不支持bulk-cone奇点；(2) AdS3双冲击波几何中本应出现的bulk-cone奇点在CFT计算中被指数压制而平滑化；(3) 奇点消解源于量子引力中bulk几何的固有模糊性（fuzziness）。

Conclusion: Bulk-cone奇点是经典/大N极限下的理想化特征，其在有限GN（即量子引力）下必然被抹除；这为全息因果结构的量子修正提供了严格约束，并支持‘量子模糊’作为量子引力中几何概念的基本属性。

Abstract: Bulk-cone singularities in holographic correlators encode propagation along bulk null geodesics. The prototypical example is an AdS black hole, where incoming null geodesics can wind around the photon sphere and return to the boundary. These singularities appear in real time in the thermal two-point function at finite volume. We show that conformal field theories dual to gravitational theories at finite $G_N$ cannot have such singularities. We rule out these singularities both for individual microstates and for the thermal state. The main thrust of the argument is to invoke an analytic continuation in the temperature. As a microscopic example, we construct an AdS$_3$ geometry with two colliding shocks which exhibits a bulk cone singularity. We show by an explicit calculation in the CFT that this singularity is smoothed out at finite $G_N$. Qualitatively, the singularity resolution is a consequence of the fuzziness of the bulk solution in quantum gravity.

</details>


### [127] [On Born-Infeld-type higher-spin interactions and supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31652)
*Nikita Zaigraev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了四维平直时空中质量为零的高自旋场(s,s1,s2)的Born-Infeld型三次相互作用顶点，具有s+s1+s2个导数，满足洛伦兹协变性；该顶点由单手征高自旋Weyl张量构造的守恒高自旋流及其迹表示，并推广至N=1与N=2超对称情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建洛伦兹协变、规范不变且可系统推广的高自旋场Born-Infeld型相互作用，弥补传统Berends-Burgers-van Dam流在手征结构与超对称延拓方面的不足。

Method: 从一般试探解出发，推导守恒流系数所满足的递推关系；通过剔除平凡（伪）顶点确定唯一非平凡守恒流；利用单手征Weyl张量C构造流J∼∂C∂C；再分别在N=1超场形式与N=2调和超空间中实现超对称延拓。

Result: 得到s≤s1+s2范围内以高自旋Weyl张量表出的显式规范不变三次顶点；导出N=1下整数超自旋s的守恒超流（双线性于超Weyl张量），并验证其与Gates-Koutrolikos结果一致；在N=2调和超空间中构造主高自旋超流，其系数由守恒方程唯一确定，且同粒子顶点不消失。

Conclusion: 本工作为Born-Infeld型高自旋相互作用及其超对称推广提供了系统、协变、手征清晰的构造框架，统一处理N=0,1,2情形，并揭示N=2下同粒子耦合的非消失特性。

Abstract: We develop a simple Lorentz-covariant construction of the Born--Infeld-type cubic interactions $(s, s_1, s_2)$ of massless higher-spin fields with $s + s_1 + s_2$ derivatives in four-dimensiona flat space. For arbitrary spins $s \ge s_1 \ge s_2$, the vertex is formulated in terms of a conserved higher-spin current and its trace. In contrast to the famous Berends--Burgers--van Dam currents, these currents are constructed from higher-spin Weyl tensors of a single chirality, schematically $J \sim \partial C \partial C$. Starting with a general ansatz, we derive recurrence relations fixing the current coefficients, identify the unique non-trivial conserved current after eliminating trivial (fake) vertices, and show that in the range $s \le s_1 + s_2$ the interaction admits a manifestly gauge-invariant representation in terms of higher-spin Weyl tensors. The construction is then extended to $\mathcal{N}=1$ and $\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetry using the corresponding off-shell superfield formalisms. In the $\mathcal{N}=1$ case, we obtain the general conserved integer superspin $s$ supercurrent with manifest supersymmetry, which is bilinear in the Weyl supertensors of superspins $Y_1$ and $Y_2$. For identical superfields, $Y_1 = Y_2$, this supercurrent vanishes for odd $s$ and is in agreement with earlier results by Gates and Koutrolikos. In the $\mathcal{N}=2$ case, using harmonic superspace, we construct the principal higher-spin supercurrent whose coefficients are uniquely fixed by the conservation equations. In contrast to the $\mathcal{N}=0,1$ cases, for identical $\mathcal{N}=2$ superfields, the corresponding vertex does not vanish. The results provide a systematic framework for Born--Infeld-type higher-spin interactions and their supersymmetric extensions.

</details>


### [128] [Half-Completeness from Standard Gravitational Dispersion Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31665)
*Lukas W. Lindwasser,Laurentiu Rodina,Justinas Rumbutis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨量子引力中完备性假设的推论，通过色散关系、正定性和交叉对称性分析，证明了在无高能截断假设下存在‘半完备性’：即对于外线无质量SU(2)同位旋J，交换谱必须包含所有j≤J或至少一个j>J；并构造了J=1时仅含j=0,1交换的显式振幅，证伪了交叉奇振幅高能界为必要条件；对J≥2，排除了一类弦启发Euler振幅中仅含低自旋谱的可能性；最后证明任意紧致内对称群下标量物质必交换无界洛伦兹自旋的单态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验完备性假设是否可由标准S矩阵框架（色散关系、正定性、交叉对称性）独立推出，特别是澄清此前依赖高能交叉奇振幅界所证完备性的逻辑必要性。

Method: 运用两次减色散关系（twice-subtracted dispersion relations）、振幅正定性（positivity bounds）与交叉对称性（crossing symmetry），结合SU(2)同位旋表示理论及Euler型振幅模型分析。

Result: 1) 对质量为零的SU(2)同位旋J外线，导出‘半完备性’定理：交换谱必含全部j≤J或至少一个j>J；2) J=1时构造满足所有标准约束但违反交叉奇高能界的振幅，证伪该界之必然性；3) J≥2时，在一类弦启发Euler振幅中排除纯低自旋谱；4) 对任意紧致内对称群，证明必存在无界洛伦兹自旋的标量单态交换。

Conclusion: 完备性假设不能仅由标准S矩阵公理（不含额外高能界）推出；‘半完备性’是更弱但普适的约束；无界洛伦兹自旋单态交换是紧致内对称性下的严格推论。

Abstract: The completeness hypothesis states that quantum gravity realizes every representation allowed by its gauge group. Previous S-matrix arguments used the graviton pole to constrain exchanged representations, including a proof of completeness by imposing an additional high-energy bound on crossing-odd amplitudes. We investigate what follows without this assumption, using standard twice-subtracted dispersion relations, positivity, and crossing symmetry. For external massless SU(2) isospin $J$, we prove ``half-completeness'': either massive exchanges include every isospin $j\le J$, or at least one $j>J$ is required. At $J=1$, we construct an explicit amplitude with only $j=0,1$ exchanges that satisfies these conditions but violates the crossing-odd bound. This establishes that the additional high-energy assumption does not follow from the standard framework. For $J\ge2$, however, we show that lower-only spectra cannot be realized within a class of string-inspired Euler amplitudes, leaving their existence an open question. More generally, for scalar matter transforming under any compact internal symmetry group, we prove that massive singlets of unbounded Lorentz spin must be exchanged.

</details>
